# AutoSpoofVN — UI/UX Redesign Audit (Phase 1)

> **Ảnh chụp lịch sử.** Tài liệu này mô tả mã nguồn *trước* đợt thiết kế lại và không
> được cập nhật theo mã hiện tại. Giữ nguyên là có chủ ý: công dụng của nó là danh sách
> đối chiếu cho cam kết "không mất tính năng nào".
>
> Những hệ thống nó nhắc tới mà nay đã **bị xóa**: `DeviceManager`, `SelfPairingManager`,
> `ConnectionRecovery`, `DeviceTransport`, `DeviceManagerView`, `SettingsView`,
> `OnboardingViewV2`, `DiagnosticsV2View`, và toàn bộ đường DVT/FFI — chúng biến mất cùng
> thư viện FFI theo yêu cầu của người dùng. Các mục liên quan ở bảng dưới không còn là
> tính năng phải giữ.

Sinh tu 10 agent doc song song toan bo repo. Moi muc deu co tham chieu `file:line`.
Day la **ban kiem ke truoc khi thiet ke lai** — hop dong zero-feature-loss.

| Cum | Tinh nang | Rui ro | API cong khai | Luong state |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Views: Main + Dashboard + Onboarding + Settings | 75 | 24 | 20 | 20 |
| Views: RouteStudio + ScenarioStudio + Bookmarks + History | 73 | 24 | 28 | 12 |
| Views: WorldTravel + FlightHUD + RoutineStudio + Diagnostics | 81 | 33 | 26 | 13 |
| Views: ShadowrocketSetup + LocationCloakGuide (BypassTroubleshoot) + DeviceManager + ShadowrocketBanner | 68 | 19 | 23 | 14 |
| Design layer + Resources + localization + Info.plist + entitlements | 22 | 15 | 14 | 6 |
| Engines: RouteProvider + RouteSimulator + ScenarioEngine + MotionEngine + HeadingEngine | 61 | 32 | 41 | 13 |
| Engines: FlightManager + RoutineManager + AirportRepository + HistoryManager (+ ReplayEngine, co-located in HistoryManager.swift) | 48 | 25 | 32 | 18 |
| Engines: DeviceManager + DeviceTransport + SelfPairingManager + ConnectionRecovery + CoordinateServer + ShadowrocketManager | 56 | 20 | 40 | 25 |
| Engines: BackgroundKeeper + LiveActivityManager + AppRecoveryManager + TimerAudit + DeterministicRandom + AppLogger | 37 | 23 | 21 | 17 |
| Widgets (Live Activity / Dynamic Island) + Persistence + Migration + Tests | 42 | 22 | 16 | 18 |
| **Tong** | **563** | **237** | **261** | **156** |

---

## 1. Rui ro nghiem trong (severity: high)

Day la nhung diem MOT BAN THIET KE LAI DE LAM HONG NHAT.

### Views: Main + Dashboard + Onboarding + Settings

**H1.** Any tap on the map immediately hijacks GPS. MainViewV2's .onTapGesture calls setManualLocation, which starts a .manual session (priority 100) and steals control from a running route/scenario/flight with no confirmation and no undo. A stray tap during a long simulation silently kills it.

> `MainViewV2.swift:191-195 → SimulationCoordinator.swift:211-216, arbitration at :66-68`

**H2.** RoutineStudioView is completely unreachable: the sheet is attached at MainViewV2.swift:115 but showRoutineStudio is never set true anywhere, and RoutineStudioView() has no other call site. A redesign that faithfully mirrors current wiring would silently ship the same dead screen.

> `MainViewV2.swift:37, 115`

**H3.** 13 of 14 AppSettings fields are write-only. mapStyle, mapFollowMode, map3DEnabled, trailEnabled, trailMaxPoints, autoReconnect, heartbeatIntervalSeconds, backgroundKeepAlive, appearance, showDeveloperInfo, deviceUpdateRateHz, simulationTickRateHz and defaultTravelMode are persisted but no code reads them back. `appearance` in particular does nothing — preferredColorScheme appears nowhere in the codebase. Users believe they are configuring the app; only the noise radius has any effect.

> `SettingsView.swift:34-140 vs. verified absence of consumers (only PersistenceManager.swift and SettingsView.swift reference these names)`

**H4.** startSession() calls ShadowrocketManager.ensureVPNActive(), which can call openShadowrocketToConnect() and open the shadowrocket:// URL — so starting a manual location (including a plain map tap) can eject the user out of the app into Shadowrocket.

> `SimulationCoordinator.swift:116-117 → ShadowrocketManager.swift:113-127`

**H5.** There is no way to reach halt() / restore-real-GPS from the main screen. Stop only ends the session; Flight or Routine can immediately re-acquire and keep writing. The only halting affordance in the app is DeviceManagerView's "Xoá GPS" button.

> `SimulationCoordinator.swift:160-164 (no caller in this cluster); DeviceManagerView.swift:39`

### Views: RouteStudio + ScenarioStudio + Bookmarks + History

**H6.** RouteStudioViewModel.startSimulation() creates `let simulator = RouteSimulator()` as a local and never retains it. RouteSimulator.start() synchronously calls coordinator.startSession(source: .route) and then spawns `simulationTask = Task { [weak self] in await self?.runSimulationLoop() }`. The moment startSimulation() returns, the simulator deallocates, self is nil inside the task, and the 10 Hz loop never runs — the coordinator is left in state .running with source .route and a frozen coordinate, and nothing ever calls stopSession(). Pressing 'Bắt đầu' also dismisses the sheet immediately, so no UI remains to stop it. History recording still starts (AutoSpoofVNApp.swift:93) and will record a zero-movement session.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/RouteStudioView.swift:243-248 (with RouteSimulator.swift:26-44, 41-43)`

**H7.** BookmarksView .onDelete applies IndexSet offsets taken from `filtered` to the unfiltered `bookmarks` array. With any category chip selected or any search text entered, swiping delete removes the wrong bookmark (or crashes if filtered has more indices than… it cannot, but it definitely deletes the wrong element). Must be rewritten to delete by LocationBookmark.id.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/BookmarksView.swift:75-78 (filter at :13-21)`

**H8.** calculateRoute() routes only waypoints.first -> waypoints.last. The UI encourages adding many intermediate waypoints (tap the map, search, coloured mid-route dots at RouteStudioView.swift:92) but they are silently discarded from the calculated geometry and from the simulated path. Users lose data with no warning.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/RouteStudioView.swift:224-241`

**H9.** Saved routes are effectively write-only. saveRoute() stores waypoints + travelMode but SavedRoute.init zeroes totalDistanceMeters/estimatedDurationSeconds and leaves routeGeometry nil, and NO view in the app lists or loads SavedRoutes (only ScenarioEngine.followRoute, which requires routeGeometry and therefore always fails, and a count in DiagnosticsV2View). The 'Lưu tuyến đường' button is a feature that produces nothing the user can retrieve.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/RouteStudioView.swift:250-259; SimulationSession.swift:95-116; ScenarioEngine.swift:122-128; DiagnosticsV2View.swift:100`

**H10.** ScenarioStudioView's running-status section (pause/stop controls) is nested inside the `else` branch that requires scenarios to be non-empty, AND the whole screen is only reachable from QuickActionsBar, which MainViewV2 hides while coordinator.state.isActive. Once a scenario is running and the sheet is closed, there is no way back in to stop it except MainViewV2's global stop. Additionally, engine.pause() sets isRunning = false, which hides the very section containing the (non-existent) resume control — ScenarioEngine.resume() is unreachable from any UI.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/ScenarioStudioView.swift:24-48 with MainViewV2.swift:69-77; ScenarioEngine.swift:51-66`

**H11.** Every scenario step is created with hardcoded constants and there is NO parameter editing UI at all: setLocation is always Hanoi 21.0285/105.8542, moveTo always 21.03/105.85 @30 km/h, wait 60 s, dwell 300 s, randomNearby 200 m/600 s, changeSpeed 40 km/h. Five of the eleven ScenarioAction cases (followRoute, changeTravelMode, loop, pause, resume) cannot be created from the UI at all, although stepDetail() renders them.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/ScenarioStudioView.swift:168-187; SimulationSession.swift:147-158`

**H12.** ReplayEngine.play() and ScenarioEngine.start() both depend on coordinator.acquire() succeeding, and both fail SILENTLY when a higher-priority source holds control (.replay = 20 is the lowest of all six sources; .manual = 100 set by any bookmark tap wins forever until released). Tapping the replay button while a manual location is active does literally nothing with no feedback.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/HistoryManager.swift:136; ScenarioEngine.swift:30-33; SimulationCoordinator.swift:65-77`

### Views: WorldTravel + FlightHUD + RoutineStudio + Diagnostics

**H13.** RoutineStudioView is dead UI: the sheet is declared but $showRoutineStudio is never set true, so the entire routine-scheduling surface (toggle, locations, schedule CRUD, editor) is unreachable in the shipping app. A redesign that only 'restyles' this screen will still ship it unreachable; a redesign that deletes it as 'unused' will delete a genuinely intended feature.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/MainViewV2.swift:37,115 (only two occurrences of showRoutineStudio)`

**H14.** FlightHUDView is dead code — never instantiated anywhere. Its two unique interactive features (the 6-step time-warp menu and 'Huỷ bay') therefore have no other UI equivalent: there is currently NO way for a user to change flight time warp or cancel a flight from the running app. Redesign must treat wiring this in as restoring a lost capability, not as new scope.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/FlightHUDView.swift:5 (declaration with zero call sites)`

**H15.** Custom RoutineSchedule records are write-only. RoutineStudioView is the sole reader/writer of documents/routines.json; no engine (RoutineManager, ScenarioEngine, RouteSimulator) ever loads them. RoutineManager.evaluateCurrentTimeSchedule() runs a hardcoded Vietnam-hours switch instead. The whole editor UI persists data that never affects behaviour.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/RoutineStudioView.swift:8,83,137 vs AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/RoutineManager.swift:94-145`

**H16.** LocationRow accepts an onUpdate closure wired to RoutineManager.updateLocation(for:coordinate:) but never calls it — the row has no Button, no onTapGesture, no NavigationLink. Editing home/work/cafe from Routine Studio is impossible despite the plumbing existing.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/RoutineStudioView.swift:148-168 (onUpdate declared at :153, unused in body :155-167)`

**H17.** Flight heading is structurally broken. FlightHUDView:41 and DiagnosticsV2View:73 read coordinator.telemetry.headingDegrees, but only SimulationCoordinator.submit(coordinate:from:speedKmh:headingDegrees:) writes it, and FlightManager/RoutineManager write GPS through SpoofEngine.shared.submit instead. syncFromLegacyEngine bridges coordinate/speed/altitude only. Heading is permanently 0° N during flights and routines.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Coordinator/SimulationCoordinator.swift:279-336 (syncFromLegacyEngine) vs FlightManager.swift:276-300`

**H18.** DiagnosticsV2View performs four synchronous JSON file decodes (routes, scenarios, bookmarks, history) inside its view body on every render pass, on the main thread. History in particular can grow unbounded (appendHistory inserts at 0). Any redesign that increases render frequency (animations, live telemetry) multiplies this cost.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/DiagnosticsV2View.swift:100-103`

### Views: ShadowrocketSetup + LocationCloakGuide (BypassTroubleshoot) + DeviceManager + ShadowrocketBanner

**H19.** ShadowrocketBanner's `onOpenSetup: () -> Void` (line 12) is declared but NEVER called anywhere in the file (verified: exactly 1 occurrence). MainViewV2:105-107 passes `{ showShadowrocket = true }`, so the intended "tap the banner to open the full setup guide" path is dead. Users can only take the single primary action; there is no way to reach ShadowrocketSetupView from the banner.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/ShadowrocketBanner.swift:12 (declared) vs :14-47 (body never uses it)`

**H20.** ShadowrocketSetupView never touches ShadowrocketManager. installLive()/installGitHub() (:493-503) only set the local @AppStorage bypass_step1_module and never call ShadowrocketManager.markModuleImported(). Result: a user who completes the whole 5-step guide still sees the banner stuck on "Import module", and ShadowrocketManager.isReady never becomes true. Two disjoint persistence namespaces (bypass_step1..5 vs autospoof_shadowrocket_module_imported / _setup_done) describe the same setup.

> `ShadowrocketSetupView.swift:16-20, 493-503 vs ShadowrocketManager.swift:50-51, 136-140`

**H21.** CoordinateServer was just changed on disk (uncommitted): @MainActor moved off the class onto start/stop/updateCoordinate only. The stored coordinate fields (latitude, longitude, accuracy, speed, heading) are now written on the MainActor by updateCoordinate but read on the background `queue` inside handleConnection → coordResponse/jsResponse/statusResponse. That is an unsynchronized data race on every HTTP request. Any redesign that adds more coordinate writes will make it worse.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/CoordinateServer.swift:18, 29-33, 79-86, 90-124 (working tree, uncommitted)`

**H22.** DeviceManagerView calls `device.generateDiagnostics()` inline in the View body (line 100). Every re-render performs a synchronous FileManager.fileExists check (SelfPairingManager.hasSavedPairing) and reads BackgroundKeeper.shared / SpoofEngine.shared, neither of which this view observes. So the 4 DiagRows do main-thread I/O on every layout pass AND still never live-update when BackgroundKeeper changes.

> `DeviceManagerView.swift:100-104 → DeviceManager.swift:113-128 → SelfPairingManager.swift:75-77`

### Design layer + Resources + localization + Info.plist + entitlements

**H23.** No asset catalog exists anywhere in the repo. `find . -name "*.xcassets" -o -name "*.appiconset" -o -name "*.colorset"` returns nothing, and project.yml sets no ASSETCATALOG_COMPILER_APPICON_NAME / GLOBAL_ACCENT_COLOR_NAME. The app therefore ships with no app icon, no AccentColor, and no custom images — every glyph in the UI is an SF Symbol. Any redesign that introduces artwork, a branded launch screen, or a custom accent must create Assets.xcassets AND add the corresponding build settings to project.yml, or the build silently ships iconless.

> `D:/ToolGN/AutoSpoofVN/project.yml:82-101 (no asset-catalog settings) and D:/ToolGN/AutoSpoofVN/AutoSpoofVN/Resources/ (no .xcassets)`

**H24.** Localization is entirely fake. Both en.lproj and vi.lproj carry 66 well-formed key/value pairs, but there are ZERO NSLocalizedString / String(localized:) / LocalizedStringKey / Bundle.localizedString call sites in the whole repo (verified by grep over every .swift file). All UI copy is hardcoded Vietnamese literals in the view files, and L10n is a plain String enum, not a localization shim. A redesign brief that says "the app is already localized" is wrong; adopting these strings means rewriting every literal in 16 view files.

> `D:/ToolGN/AutoSpoofVN/AutoSpoofVN/Resources/en.lproj/Localizable.strings:1-67 and vi.lproj/Localizable.strings:1-67, vs. AppDesign.swift:250-278`

**H25.** get-task-allow=true lives in the single entitlements file that project.yml wires to ALL configs (Debug, Debug-FFI and Release alike — CODE_SIGN_ENTITLEMENTS is set at target `base` level, with no per-config override). Archiving for TestFlight/App Store with get-task-allow=true is rejected. Both schemes archive with `config: Release`.

> `D:/ToolGN/AutoSpoofVN/AutoSpoofVN/Resources/AutoSpoofVN.entitlements:5-6 wired at project.yml:88; archive configs at project.yml:40-41 and :59-60`

### Engines: RouteProvider + RouteSimulator + ScenarioEngine + MotionEngine + HeadingEngine

**H26.** RouteSimulator is never retained. RouteStudioViewModel.startSimulation() creates `let simulator = RouteSimulator()`, calls start(), and returns — the object deallocates at the end of the function. The simulation Task captures `[weak self]` (RouteSimulator.swift:41-43), so `self?` is nil and runSimulationLoop never executes. Net effect: coordinator.startSession(source:.route) DID run (session .running, device update timer started, Shadowrocket VPN triggered) but not one coordinate is ever submitted and nothing ever calls stopSession(). The route feature is dead on arrival and leaks a running session. The new UI MUST own the simulator (@StateObject or a RouteSimulationManager.shared).

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/RouteStudioView.swift:244-249 together with AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/Route/RouteSimulator.swift:41-43`

**H27.** RouteSimulator.pause() terminates instead of pausing. It sets isRunning = false; the loop condition at RouteSimulator.swift:82 includes `isRunning`, so the loop exits, falls into the `if !Task.isCancelled` branch (line 121) and calls stopSession() with progress forced to 1.0. resume() then no-ops because coordinator.resumeSession() guards on state == .paused and stopSession() already set state = .idle. Any pause button the redesign adds will silently kill the run and report 100% complete.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/Route/RouteSimulator.swift:46-49 vs 82 and 121-126`

**H28.** ScenarioEngine.pause() releases the GPS source. pause() clears isRunning; executeSteps returns at the next step boundary (line 100); executeScenario's `if !Task.isCancelled` branch then calls coordinator.release(.scenario) (line 90). A paused scenario therefore surrenders arbitration, and any lower-priority source (routine 40, replay 20) can take over while the user believes the scenario is merely paused. Adding a resume button in the redesign will make this reachable far more often.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/Scenario/ScenarioEngine.swift:51-54 and 86-92`

**H29.** Both engines ignore the Bool returned by coordinator.submit(). submit() returns false when isHalted or when a higher-priority source holds arbitration, but RouteSimulator.swift:96 and ScenarioEngine.swift:116/160/192 discard it and keep looping at 10 Hz. After a halt() or a priority steal, the simulation loops burn CPU forever with no user-visible indication and never self-terminate.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/Route/RouteSimulator.swift:96-101; AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/Scenario/ScenarioEngine.swift:116, 160, 192-195`

**H30.** ScenarioAction.followRoute is functionally dead. It reads SavedRoute.routeGeometry, but that property is never assigned anywhere in the codebase — RouteStudioViewModel.saveRoute() constructs SavedRoute(name:waypoints:travelMode:) whose init leaves routeGeometry nil (SimulationSession.swift:105-115). The `if let geometry` guard therefore always fails and the step silently does nothing. A redesign that adds a followRoute step picker will ship a step that never runs unless saveRoute is fixed to persist the geometry.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/Scenario/ScenarioEngine.swift:122-128 vs AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/RouteStudioView.swift:251-260 and AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Models/SimulationSession.swift:104-115`

**H31.** O(n) distance recomputation inside the 10 Hz main-actor loop. totalRouteDistance() and distanceFromIndex() each walk the whole remaining coordinate array (with an awaited actor hop per haversine call), and totalRouteDistance() is called three more times per tick at lines 104-105. With resample producing up to 20 000 points this is tens of thousands of cross-actor calls per second on the MainActor — visible UI stutter on long routes. Precompute a cumulative-distance array.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/Route/RouteSimulator.swift:85, 104-105, 131-145`

### Engines: FlightManager + RoutineManager + AirportRepository + HistoryManager (+ ReplayEngine, co-located in HistoryManager.swift)

**H32.** KHÔNG có cách nào dừng chuyến bay bằng UI hiện tại. FlightHUDView là view DUY NHẤT gọi stopFlight() và flightTimeWarpMultiplier, nhưng `FlightHUDView` không được khởi tạo ở bất kỳ file nào (đã grep toàn repo). coordinator.stopSession() KHÔNG gọi FlightManager.stopFlight(), nên flightTimer 1s vẫn tiếp tục submit toạ độ xuống SpoofEngine sau khi user bấm 'Dừng' trên TelemetryPanel.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/FlightHUDView.swift:5 (không có call site); AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/FlightManager.swift:473; SimulationCoordinator.swift:137-157`

**H33.** Routine Studio KHÔNG mở được. `showRoutineStudio` được khai báo (MainViewV2.swift:37) và gắn sheet (MainViewV2.swift:115) nhưng KHÔNG BAO GIỜ được set true — không có nút nào trong mapControls hay QuickActionsBar. Toàn bộ chế độ chu trình 24/7 (toggleAutoRoutine) hiện không truy cập được từ UI.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/MainViewV2.swift:37,115,244-252`

**H34.** Chỉnh sửa Nhà/Công ty/Quán cà phê là tính năng CHẾT: RoutineStudioView truyền closure onUpdate cho LocationRow nhưng thân LocationRow chỉ là HStack hiển thị, không có Button/onTapGesture nào gọi onUpdate. RoutineManager.updateLocation(for:coordinate:) không có call site thực thi được.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/RoutineStudioView.swift:47-58 và :148-168`

**H35.** Mất vĩnh viễn địa điểm thật của người dùng nếu app bị kill giữa chuyến du lịch. isTravelOverrideActive KHÔNG persist, trong khi home/work/cafe/bookmarks giả ĐÃ được persist qua didSet. Sau khi khởi động lại, engine coi toạ độ khách sạn/điểm tham quan là địa điểm thật; chuyến đi kế tiếp gọi beginTravelOverride() sẽ ghi đè bản backup bằng dữ liệu giả → không còn đường lùi.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/RoutineManager.swift:388,395-405,15-26; FlightManager.swift:336-351`

**H36.** endTravelOverride() được gọi vô điều kiện trong stopFlight(), kể cả với chuyến bay A→B thường (chưa từng gọi beginTravelOverride). Nếu UserDefaults còn backup cũ (từ một phiên bị kill), nó sẽ khôi phục dữ liệu cũ đè lên địa điểm hiện tại của user.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/FlightManager.swift:483 → RoutineManager.swift:408-417`

**H37.** Mọi record lịch sử bị gán SAI nguồn. MainViewV2 gọi coordinator.stopSession() (giải phóng activeSource, xoá telemetry) TRƯỚC historyManager.stopRecording(); bootstrap sink cũng chỉ chạy stopRecording sau khi state đã về .idle. Kết quả: `coordinator.activeSource ?? .manual` luôn ra .manual, sessionId là UUID mới, travelMode luôn .driving, routeName luôn nil → dòng lịch sử nào cũng hiển thị 'Thủ công'.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/MainViewV2.swift:84-87; AutoSpoofVN/Sources/AutoSpoofVNApp.swift:97-99; AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/HistoryManager.swift:53,70-77`

**H38.** Timer của FlightManager dùng Timer.scheduledTimer (RunLoop .default) — TẤT CẢ dừng lại khi người dùng cuộn/kéo bản đồ hoặc list. RoutineManager đã sửa lỗi này cho moveStepTimer (RunLoop.main.add ... forMode: .common) nhưng routineTimer 60s vẫn ở .default. Vị trí sẽ 'đóng băng' khi user tương tác UI — càng nghiêm trọng với redesign nhiều scroll view.

> `FlightManager.swift:247,368,461 (scheduledTimer); RoutineManager.swift:148 (scheduledTimer) vs :239,316 (.common); HistoryManager.swift:32 (scheduledTimer)`

**H39.** I/O file trên main thread: stopRecording/deleteRecord/clearAll đều gọi PersistenceManager.saveHistory (JSONEncoder + Data.write đồng bộ) từ @MainActor. Với 500 record × tối đa 5000 điểm replay, file có thể hàng chục MB → giật/đóng băng UI. init cũng loadHistory() đồng bộ khi app khởi động.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/HistoryManager.swift:20,84,93,98; PersistenceManager.swift:160-167`

**H40.** toggleAutoWorldOdyssey() khi TẮT không dừng flightTimer, không đặt isFlying = false, không release(.flight) — chỉ dừng tourTimer/transitionTimer. Bấm tắt giữa chuyến bay thì máy bay vẫn tiếp tục bay.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/FlightManager.swift:179-183`

**H41.** FlightManager và RoutineManager bỏ qua SimulationCoordinator hoàn toàn khi ghi toạ độ: dùng SpoofEngine.shared.submit(...). Hệ quả: noiseConfig của coordinator KHÔNG áp cho flight/routine (chỉ có jitter của SpoofEngine), session/telemetry.elapsedTime không cập nhật, và state v2 chỉ được suy diễn ngược qua $isSimulating. Redesign nếu chuyển sang coordinator.submit sẽ bị NHIỄU HAI LẦN (coordinator.applyNoise rồi SpoofEngine.applyLocation lại jitter tiếp).

> `FlightManager.swift:219,282,353,387-444,463; RoutineManager.swift:161,171,235,291,308; SimulationCoordinator.swift:176,198,279-318`

**H42.** Ghép chéo hai singleton: FlightManager GHI trực tiếp vào RoutineManager.shared.currentState/.statusDescription/.currentSpeedKmh/.homeLocation/.workLocation/.cafeLocation/.bookmarks. Tách hai màn hình trong redesign mà không giữ cầu nối này sẽ mất toàn bộ text trạng thái chuyến bay trên Live Activity/Widget.

> `FlightManager.swift:182,221-223,292-293,313-315,336-356,384-444,480-483`

### Engines: DeviceManager + DeviceTransport + SelfPairingManager + ConnectionRecovery + CoordinateServer + ShadowrocketManager

**H43.** Two competing device-state sources of truth. DeviceManager.connectionState and SimulationCoordinator.deviceState are computed independently from the same SpoofEngine publishers (DeviceManager.swift:159-184 vs SimulationCoordinator.swift:344/348/359). The dashboard header reads the Coordinator's copy, DeviceManagerView reads DeviceManager's copy — they can disagree (e.g. DeviceManager latches .error from lastFFIError and never clears it on a later success, while the Coordinator does not). A redesign that unifies on one of them will silently change what the header shows.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/Device/DeviceManager.swift:175-183 vs AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Coordinator/SimulationCoordinator.swift:340-360`

**H44.** Settings "Tự kết nối lại" toggle and the Heartbeat slider are dead controls. SettingsView binds them to PersistenceManager.AppSettings (SettingsView.swift:67, :74), the Save button only applies noiseConfig (SettingsView.swift:170-174), and DeviceManager never reads AppSettings. autoReconnect is therefore permanently true and startHeartbeat() always uses its 20s default. A redesign that 'preserves' these controls as-is preserves a lie.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/SettingsView.swift:67-74 + AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/Device/DeviceManager.swift:19,77`

**H45.** CoordinateServer data race on the coordinate fields. The class is no longer @MainActor (uncommitted working-tree change); latitude/longitude/accuracy/speed/heading are written on the main actor by updateCoordinate() but read on the com.autospoof.vn.coordserver queue inside coordResponse/jsResponse/statusResponse, and requestCount is read there too while being written on the main actor. Under Swift 6 strict concurrency this will not compile; under Swift 5 it is a live race.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/CoordinateServer.swift:18, 29-33, 80-86, 90-124, 128-197`

**H46.** Starting a simulation can eject the user from the app. SimulationCoordinator.startSession unconditionally calls ShadowrocketManager.ensureVPNActive(), which may call importModule() or openShadowrocketToConnect() — both switch to another app. Every Start affordance in the redesigned UI inherits this behaviour with no confirmation step.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Coordinator/SimulationCoordinator.swift:117 → AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/ShadowrocketManager.swift:113-127`

**H47.** Two independent, divergent 'module imported' truths. ShadowrocketManager.isModuleImported (UserDefaults autospoof_shadowrocket_module_imported, set only by ShadowrocketManager.importModule's success callback) vs ShadowrocketSetupView's @AppStorage bypass_step1_module (set unconditionally by installLive()/installGitHub()). Installing from the setup sheet ticks the checklist but leaves the dashboard banner insisting the module is missing.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/ShadowrocketManager.swift:136-140 vs AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/ShadowrocketSetupView.swift:493-504`

**H48.** ConnectionRecovery and ShadowrocketManager only exist because bootstrap() touches `_ = X.shared` (AutoSpoofVNApp.swift:104, :107), and bootstrap only runs after onboarding completes. Refactoring the app entry point can silently disarm auto-recovery and the Shadowrocket banner with no compile error.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/AutoSpoofVNApp.swift:44-51, 103-107`

### Engines: BackgroundKeeper + LiveActivityManager + AppRecoveryManager + TimerAudit + DeterministicRandom + AppLogger

**H49.** BackgroundKeeper publishes @Published changes from a BACKGROUND queue. startAudio() dispatches configureAndPlayAudio() onto DispatchQueue.global(qos:.userInitiated) (lines 88-96) and that method assigns isAudioRunning/audioError (lines 112-117). DiagnosticsV2View holds it as @StateObject (DiagnosticsV2View.swift:7), so SwiftUI receives objectWillChange off the main thread -> 'Publishing changes from background threads is not allowed' runtime warning and potential UI corruption/crash. SpoofEngine avoids this only because it re-publishes with .receive(on: DispatchQueue.main) (SpoofEngine.swift:119, 123). The redesign will hit this the moment any new view observes BackgroundKeeper directly.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/BackgroundKeeper.swift:88-119 vs. Views/DiagnosticsV2View.swift:7`

**H50.** BackgroundKeeper.stop() is unreachable — SpoofEngine.stopBackgroundKeepAlive() (SpoofEngine.swift:233) has zero call sites, so audio playback, background location updates and the 20 s heartbeat Timer run for the entire app lifetime with no way to turn them off. Battery drain and App Review risk. The 'Giữ app sống nền' Settings toggle looks like the off-switch but is not wired to anything.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/BackgroundKeeper.swift:67 + Engine/SpoofEngine.swift:233 + Views/SettingsView.swift:81`

**H51.** stop() never calls stopMonitoringSignificantLocationChanges(). enableBackgroundLocation() starts it (line 200) but stop() only stops standard updates (lines 72-74). Significant-location monitoring persists across app termination and will keep relaunching the app indefinitely with no way to cancel from inside the app.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/BackgroundKeeper.swift:195-202 vs. 67-78`

**H52.** LiveActivityManager.endActivity() has zero call sites. Once started at bootstrap the Live Activity never ends — it keeps showing (and being updated every 800 ms) after the user stops the simulation, after the session completes, and until iOS's 8-hour limit. Also, if the user manually dismisses the Live Activity, currentActivity stays non-nil so the manager keeps calling update() on a dead activity and never re-requests a new one.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/LiveActivityManager.swift:83-108`

**H53.** @MainActor LiveActivityManager reads four NON-@MainActor singletons (SpoofEngine, RoutineManager, FlightManager are plain final classes; only SimulationCoordinator and DeviceManager are @MainActor) and subscribes to their publishers in init. Under Swift 6 / strict concurrency this is a hard error, and today it is a genuine data-race surface: FlightManager mutates RoutineManager.shared.currentSpeedKmh/statusDescription from its 1 s timer (FlightManager.swift:223, 292-293) while LiveActivityManager reads them.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/LiveActivityManager.swift:27-30, 110-125; Engine/SpoofEngine.swift:38; Engine/RoutineManager.swift:6; Engine/FlightManager.swift:5`

**H54.** The willResignActive checkpoint, the willEnterForeground reconnect, the recovery alert and the bootstrap onAppear/onChange are ALL attached to the anonymous root Group in AutoSpoofVNApp.body (lines 44-72). A UI redesign that swaps the root container is very likely to drop one of these four silently — no compile error, just lost crash recovery / lost reconnect / no background keep-alive.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/AutoSpoofVNApp.swift:30-73`

### Widgets (Live Activity / Dynamic Island) + Persistence + Migration + Tests

**H55.** FAILING TEST: testSmoothHeadingNoJump asserts the result is > 340, but MotionEngine.smoothHeading(from: 350, to: 10, factor: 0.5) returns exactly 0.0 (350 + 20*0.5 = 360, then the `if result >= 360 { result -= 360 }` normalisation at MotionEngine.swift:135 wraps it to 0). I verified the arithmetic numerically. The production code is correct — the assertion is wrong. Anyone running the suite before/after the redesign will see a red test and may 'fix' the engine instead of the test.

> `AutoSpoofVNTests/SimulationTests.swift:45-49 vs AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/Motion/MotionEngine.swift:129-137`

**H56.** The Live Activity is started at app bootstrap and NEVER ended: endActivity() has zero call sites in the entire repo. After onboarding, the Lock Screen banner appears showing 'Sẵn sàng' / 0 km/h with no simulation running, and it survives stopSession, halt, and app termination until the system's 8h/12h limit.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/LiveActivityManager.swift:80 (start at bootstrap via AutoSpoofVNApp.swift:80) and :102-108 (endActivity, unreferenced)`

**H57.** LiveActivityManager reaches four singletons directly (SimulationCoordinator, SpoofEngine, RoutineManager, FlightManager) instead of receiving them. The three-way branch at :42-60 encodes the source-priority policy a SECOND time, independently of SimulationSource.priority. If the redesign changes which source wins, the widget will disagree with the app until this branch is edited too.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/LiveActivityManager.swift:27-30, :42-70`

**H58.** PersistenceManager does synchronous, blocking JSON encode/decode of whole files, and it is called from SwiftUI view bodies and view initialisers. DiagnosticsV2View performs FOUR full-file decodes inside its body, so every re-render re-reads routes + scenarios + bookmarks + history (history can be multi-MB because SimulationRecord embeds up to 5000 replay points).

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/DiagnosticsV2View.swift:100-103; also BookmarksView.swift:7, RoutineStudioView.swift:8, SettingsView.swift:5`

**H59.** Nine of the fourteen AppSettings fields are persisted but never read by any engine: defaultTravelMode, deviceUpdateRateHz, simulationTickRateHz, mapFollowMode, map3DEnabled, mapStyle, trailEnabled, trailMaxPoints, heartbeatIntervalSeconds, appearance, showDeveloperInfo. Only noiseConfig is applied (SettingsView.swift:172). The 'Tự kết nối lại' toggle is especially deceptive: the real behaviour is DeviceManager.shared.autoReconnect, a completely separate property.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Persistence/PersistenceManager.swift:86-101 vs AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/SettingsView.swift:34-133; DeviceManager.swift:19`

**H60.** SavedRoute.routeGeometry is declared but NEVER assigned anywhere (verified by grep — the only reads are ScenarioEngine.swift:125 and unrelated local vars in RouteStudioView). Consequently ScenarioAction.followRoute is a permanent silent no-op for every saved route. Likewise SavedRoute.totalDistanceMeters and .estimatedDurationSeconds are hardcoded to 0 in the initialiser and never updated, so every saved route displays 0 m / 0 s.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Models/SimulationSession.swift:102-115; AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/RouteStudioView.swift:250-258; AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/Scenario/ScenarioEngine.swift:123-128`

**H61.** Two parallel, incompatible source-priority enums coexist: SpoofSource (legacy, 3 cases, priorities 10/20/30, SpoofEngine.swift:12-32) and SimulationSource (v2, 6 cases, priorities 20-100, SimulationTypes.swift:7-50). Both are tested as if each were THE contract (EngineTests.swift:65-68 and SimulationTests.swift:100-104). The Live Activity reads activeSource from both (LiveActivityManager.swift:64-70). Any arbitration change must touch both enums or the widget badge and the engine will disagree.

> `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/SpoofEngine.swift:12-32 vs AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Models/SimulationTypes.swift:7-50`

_Tong cong 61 rui ro high._

---

## 2. Kiem ke tinh nang day du

Moi dong = mot kha nang nguoi dung cham vao duoc. Cot **Goi vao** la hop dong noi day cho UI moi.

### Design layer + Resources + localization + Info.plist + entitlements (22)

| # | Tinh nang | Kich hoat boi | Goi vao | Phai giu | Vi tri |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | StatusBadge — compact pill status indicator | Non-interactive display. Rendered at 11 call sites: DashboardComponents.swift:17, :23, :203; DeviceManagerView.swift:28; FlightHUDView.swift:29; HistoryView.swift:24; OnboardingViewV2.swift:134; RoutineStudioView.swift:33; ScenarioStudioView.swift:31, :111; SettingsView.swift:85 | No domain API. Pure presentational. Call sites feed it SimulationSource.displayName/.icon, sim.phase.rawValue, routine.currentState.rawValue/.icon, BackgroundKeeper.shared.isAudioRunning | init signature StatusBadge(_ text: String, color: Color = .green, icon: String? = nil) — 11 call sites use positional text + labelled color/icon. Behaviour: nil icon renders a 6pt filled Circle, non-nil icon renders an 8pt SF Symbol; background is color.opacity(0.12) in a Capsule. | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Design/AppDesign.swift:71-100 |
| 2 | AppCard — rounded surface container | Layout container. 6 call sites: LocationCloakGuideView.swift:109; ShadowrocketSetupView.swift:87, :413, :428, :453; WorldTravelViewV2.swift:211 | No domain API. Pure layout. | @ViewBuilder trailing-closure init AppCard { ... }. Hardcoded AppSpacing.lg (16) padding, AppColor.surfaceSecondary background, AppRadius.lg (14) corner radius — no parameters exist to override any of these. | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Design/AppDesign.swift:103-116 |
| 3 | MetricView — labelled telemetry value with optional unit and icon | Non-interactive display. 10 call sites: DashboardComponents.swift:35 (speed), :36 (heading), :38 (distance remaining), :41 (ETA); RouteStudioView.swift:113, :115, :117; WorldTravelViewV2.swift:214, :216, :218 | Reads SimulationCoordinator.telemetry.speedKmh, .formattedHeading, .distanceRemainingMeters and the session ETA at the DashboardComponents call sites (values are pre-formatted by the caller, not by MetricView) | init(_ label: String, value: String, unit: String? = nil, icon: String? = nil). All four telemetry readouts on the dashboard route through this one component — replacing it must keep the same value/unit split or the km/h and heading readouts break. | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Design/AppDesign.swift:119-156 |
| 4 | EmptyStateView — icon + title + message + optional CTA button | CTA button is a real user action. 4 call sites: BookmarksView.swift:27; HistoryView.swift:13; RouteStudioView.swift:376; ScenarioStudioView.swift:15 | Caller-supplied `action: (() -> Void)?` closure. At ScenarioStudioView.swift:20-21 it sets `editingScenario = Scenario(name: "Kịch bản mới")` and `showEditor = true`, i.e. it opens the scenario editor sheet. | init(icon:title:message:actionTitle:action:) with actionTitle/action both optional AND both required for the button to appear (`if let actionTitle, let action`). Losing the optional-pair semantics silently removes the CTA from all four empty states. | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Design/AppDesign.swift:159-204 |
| 5 | ErrorBanner — warning banner with Retry and Diagnostics buttons | Would render two buttons labelled "Thử lại" (line 231) and "Chẩn đoán" (line 235). ZERO call sites in the whole repo — verified by grep across all .swift files. | Caller-supplied retryAction / diagnosticsAction closures. Nothing wires them today; the obvious intended target is SimulationCoordinator.runDiagnostics(). | Nothing is currently wired, so nothing can be lost — but the intent (an inline error surface with retry + diagnostics escape hatches) is unimplemented and the redesign should decide explicitly whether to build it or delete it. Do NOT count it as an existing feature. | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Design/AppDesign.swift:207-245 |
| 6 | AccessibleMetric — VoiceOver-combined wrapper around MetricView | Never. ZERO call sites — verified by grep. MetricView is used raw everywhere instead, so telemetry values are NOT VoiceOver-combined today. | Wraps MetricView; adds .accessibilityElement(children: .combine) and a "label: value unit" accessibility label. | Nothing wired. Flag as an accessibility gap the redesign should close, not a feature to port. | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Design/Accessibility.swift:19-29 |
| 7 | ScalableText — Dynamic Type clamped text | Never. ZERO call sites — verified by grep. | None. Applies .dynamicTypeSize(.xSmall ... .accessibility3). | Nothing wired. The whole app currently uses AppFont tokens with no Dynamic Type clamping. | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Design/Accessibility.swift:32-46 |
| 8 | accessibleButton(label:hint:) — VoiceOver labels for icon-only buttons | Never. ZERO call sites — verified by grep. Every icon-only button in the app is therefore unlabelled for VoiceOver. | SwiftUI .accessibilityLabel/.accessibilityHint/.accessibilityAddTraits(.isButton) | Nothing wired. Accessibility gap, not a feature. | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Design/Accessibility.swift:6-10 |

### Engines: RouteProvider + RouteSimulator + ScenarioEngine + MotionEngine + HeadingEngine (61)

| # | Tinh nang | Kich hoat boi | Goi vao | Phai giu | Vi tri |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Resolve real-road route (MapKit MKDirections) | "Tính tuyến" button in RouteStudioView routePanel (RouteStudioView.swift:120-131) → viewModel.calculateRoute(); also automatically by RoutineManager commute legs (RoutineManager.swift:332) | await RouteProvider.shared.resolveRoute(named:from:to:transportType:speedKmh:sampleSpacingMeters:autoLoop:) -> RouteProvider.RouteResult | Must stay async/await against the actor. Caller must keep passing sampleSpacingMeters (RouteStudio uses 25 m; RoutineManager computes max(5, speed*1000/3600*2.5)) and must consume result.plan.waypoints, result.distanceMeters, result.expectedTravelTime, result.source.displayName. | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/RouteProvider.swift:133-220 |
| 2 | Automatic straight-line fallback when MapKit fails | No UI affordance — happens implicitly on MKDirections error, invalid coordinates, or a cached failure | RouteProvider.fallbackResult(...) → RouteProvider.straightLineCoordinates(from:to:spacingMeters:) | New UI must still surface result.source.displayName ("Nội suy đường thẳng") and result.usedFallback so the user knows the route is not a real road; RoutineManager.applyRoute branches on usedFallback (RoutineManager.swift:357-361). | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/RouteProvider.swift:204-219, 532-554 |
| 3 | Route source label readout | MetricView("Nguồn", …) in the route info row, visible after a route is calculated | RouteProvider.RouteResult.source.displayName ("MKDirections" \| "Nội suy đường thẳng") | Keep a UI slot for the route provenance string; it is the only user-visible signal that the route degraded to a straight line. | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/RouteProvider.swift:13-21 (displayName) consumed at RouteStudioView.swift:239 + 113 |
| 4 | Route distance + expected travel time readout | MetricView("Khoảng cách") and MetricView("Thời gian") in RouteStudioView.swift:111-118 | RouteResult.distanceMeters, RouteResult.expectedTravelTime (nil for fallback → caller derives distance / (cruise/3.6)) | expectedTravelTime is Optional and is nil on fallback; the fallback duration derivation at RouteStudioView.swift:238 must be kept or reimplemented. | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/RouteProvider.swift:25-26 consumed at RouteStudioView.swift:236-238 |

| 7 | Degenerate zero-length route short-circuit | Implicit when start and end are < 0.5 m apart | Returns RouteResult(plan: single-waypoint RoutePlan, source: .straightLineFallback, distanceMeters: 0, expectedTravelTime: 0) | Callers must tolerate a RoutePlan with a single waypoint (RouteSimulator.start bails out on count < 2, RoutineManager.applyRoute bails on count < 2). | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/RouteProvider.swift:155-168 |
| 8 | Polyline resampling to fixed spacing | Implicit on every resolved route; also directly unit-tested (AutoSpoofVNTests/RouteProviderTests.swift:25,40) | static RouteProvider.resample(_:spacingMeters:maximumPointCount:) using GeodesicMath.interpolateGreatCircle | Public static, used by tests. Spacing is the knob that sets simulation smoothness — any new UI "smoothness/quality" control must map onto sampleSpacingMeters. | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/RouteProvider.swift:272-337 |

### Views: RouteStudio + ScenarioStudio + Bookmarks + History

| State | Chu so huu | Chieu | Ai tieu thu |
|---|---|---|---|

## 4. API domain cho tang UI moi

### Views: Main + Dashboard + Onboarding + Settings

| Chu ky | File | Ngu nghia |
|---|---|---|
| `static let shared: SimulationCoordinator  // @MainActor final class, ObservableObject` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Coordinator/SimulationCoordinator.swift:16 | Singleton. All four audited views obtain it via @StateObject SimulationCoordinator.shared (MainViewV2:11, SettingsView:6) rather than the @EnvironmentObject the app injects at AutoSpoofVNApp.swift:43. |
| `func setManualLocation(_ coordinate: CLLocationCoordinate2D)` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Coordinator/SimulationCoordinator.swift:211-216 | If state == .idle, starts a .manual session first; then submits from .manual. Manual has priority 100, so it ALWAYS wins arbitration and silently steals control from a running route/scenario/flight. No coordinate validation inside — callers must range-check. |
| `func pauseSession()` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Coordinator/SimulationCoordinator.swift:123-128 | No-op unless state == .running. Sets state/.session status to .paused and invalidates the 20s device heartbeat timer. |
| `func resumeSession()` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Coordinator/SimulationCoordinator.swift:130-135 | No-op unless state == .paused. Restores .running and restarts the heartbeat timer. |
| `func stopSession()` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Coordinator/SimulationCoordinator.swift:137-157 | Guarded on state.canStop. Transitions .stopping → clears device location via legacyEngine.clearDeviceLocation() → releases activeSource → stamps endedAt/endCoordinate → state = .idle → stops heartbeat → resets telemetry to a fresh SimulationTelemetry(). Does NOT set isHalted, so other sources may resume writing. |
| `func halt()` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Coordinator/SimulationCoordinator.swift:160-164 | stopSession() + isHalted = true + legacyEngine.haltSimulation() (restores real GPS and blocks all further writes until acquire()). NOT REACHABLE from any view in this cluster — no button calls it. |
| `@discardableResult func startSession(source: SimulationSource, travelMode: TravelMode = .driving) -> SimulationSession` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Coordinator/SimulationCoordinator.swift:89-121 | Not called directly by this cluster (only indirectly via setManualLocation). Side effect worth knowing for redesign: it calls ShadowrocketManager.shared.ensureVPNActive(), which can open the Shadowrocket app and background AutoSpoofVN. |
| `@discardableResult func acquire(_ source: SimulationSource) -> Bool / func release(_ source: SimulationSource)` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Coordinator/SimulationCoordinator.swift:65-85 | Priority arbitration (manual 100 > scenario 80 > flight 60 > route 50 > routine 40 > replay 20). acquire also clears isHalted. Not called directly from this cluster's views. |
| `func runDiagnostics() -> [(String, SystemHealth.Status, String)]` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Coordinator/SimulationCoordinator.swift:368-408 | Returns 5 rows (Thiết bị, Transport, Chạy nền, Mô phỏng, Live Activity). Not called from this cluster — DiagnosticsV2View owns it. |
| `static func sampleTrail(_ trail: inout [CLLocationCoordinate2D], newPoint: CLLocationCoordinate2D, maxPoints: Int = 1000)` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/DashboardComponents.swift:216-222 | Appends then, when over maxPoints, decimates by keeping every 2nd element. Pure value helper — safe to reuse verbatim in a new view. |
| `func loadSettings() -> PersistenceManager.AppSettings / func saveSettings(_ settings: AppSettings)` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Persistence/PersistenceManager.swift:105-116 | JSON-encoded blob in UserDefaults key "autospoof_settings_v2". Decode failure silently returns defaults, so a schema change wipes user settings without warning. |
| `struct PersistenceManager.AppSettings: Codable { noiseConfig, defaultTravelMode, deviceUpdateRateHz, simulationTickRateHz, mapFollowMode, map3DEnabled, mapStyle, trailEnabled, trailMaxPoints, autoReconnect, heartbeatIntervalSeconds, backgroundKeepAlive, appearance, showDeveloperInfo }` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Persistence/PersistenceManager.swift:86-101 | SettingsView exposes 11 of the 14 fields; simulationTickRateHz and trailMaxPoints have no UI at all. Only noiseConfig is consumed at runtime. |
| `func startAutoPairing() / func cancel() / nonisolated func registerBackgroundTask()` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/SelfPairingManager.swift:82, 101, 79 | startAutoPairing is called only from OnboardingViewV2:119. cancel() has zero call sites in any view. registerBackgroundTask() is called from AutoSpoofVNApp.bootstrap():78. |
| `@Published private(set) var phase: SelfPairingManager.Phase  // .idle \| .requestingPermission \| .advertising \| .showPin(String) \| .connectingTunnel \| .success(name:model:udid:) \| .failed(String), with .description` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/SelfPairingManager.swift:24-53 | OnboardingViewV2 renders phase.description and colour-codes success/failed/idle/other. A redesign should surface .failed(String) text — today it only appears inside the generic description line. |
| `func openAppStore() / func importModule() / func openShadowrocketToConnect() / func markSetupCompleted() / func markModuleImported() / func resetSetup() / func ensureVPNActive() / func detectInstallation()` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/ShadowrocketManager.swift:76, 83, 106, 130, 136, 143, 113, 68 | ShadowrocketBanner.primaryAction() (ShadowrocketBanner.swift:83-93) calls exactly four of these in a strict order: openAppStore → importModule → openShadowrocketToConnect → markSetupCompleted. openShadowrocketToConnect opens the shadowrocket:// URL scheme and leaves the app. |
| `func startRecording(source: SimulationSource, travelMode: TravelMode) / @discardableResult func stopRecording() -> SimulationRecord?` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/HistoryManager.swift:25, 41 | stopRecording is idempotent (returns nil when there is no active record). MainViewV2:86 calls it explicitly and AutoSpoofVNApp:98 calls it again from the state sink; only one save happens. |
| `func setLocation(latitude: Double, longitude: Double) / func sendLocationToDevice(_ coord: CLLocationCoordinate2D) / func clearDeviceLocation() / func haltSimulation()` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/SpoofEngine.swift:167, 349, 358, 373 | The FFI bridge the coordinator uses. Not called directly by this cluster's views — keep it that way in the redesign. |
| `struct StatusBadge(_ text: String, color: Color = .green, icon: String? = nil) / struct MetricView(_ label: String, value: String, unit: String? = nil, icon: String? = nil)` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Design/AppDesign.swift:71-100, 119-156 | Shared primitives used across all four files. AppCard, EmptyStateView, ErrorBanner also exist but are NOT used by this cluster (available for the redesign). |
| `func safeAnimation<V: Equatable>(_ value: V, _ animation: Animation = .easeInOut) -> some View` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Design/Accessibility.swift:13-15 | Reduce-Motion-aware animation. Used only at OnboardingViewV2:29. MainViewV2's withAnimation calls (:227 implicit, :285) bypass it and do NOT respect Reduce Motion. |
| `enum L10n { start, stop, pause, resume, retry, diagnostics, ... }` | AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Design/AppDesign.swift:250-279 | Only TelemetryPanel uses L10n (pause/resume/stop at DashboardComponents.swift:65,76,86). Every other string in this cluster is a hardcoded Vietnamese literal. |
