## 7. Ghi chu tung cum

### Views: Main + Dashboard + Onboarding + Settings

MOCK / STUB / PLACEHOLDER CODE FOUND (do not mistake for real logic, and do not "fix" the intentional ones):

1. USE_IDEVICE_FFI is ENABLED in this project — project.yml:106 sets SWIFT_ACTIVE_COMPILATION_CONDITIONS: "$(inherited) USE_IDEVICE_FFI". So the real FFI paths in SpoofEngine.swift (:269, 306, 322, 333, 350, 362), SelfPairingManager.swift (:15, 114, 268) and DeviceTransport.swift (:11, 57, 93, 109, 122, 134) are live. The #else branches are the graceful-degradation fallback for builds without libidevice_ffi.a and produce user-visible strings ("Bản build thiếu thư viện Rust FFI", "Chưa kết nối: bản build chưa có libidevice_ffi.a", SpoofEngine.swift:296-301). Note that in the #else world sendLocationToDevice/clearDeviceLocation are silent no-ops, so the UI would show a running simulation writing nothing.

2. MockDeviceTransport (DeviceTransport.swift:143, transportType .mock = "Chế độ Kiểm thử (Mock)") is an intentional test double, not shipped UI. None of the four audited views reference it.

3. SettingsView.swift:155 renders the FFI ABI as a hardcoded "v3" string with no runtime source. This is a genuine placeholder.

4. SimulationCoordinator.runDiagnostics() returns a hardcoded Live Activity row (.unknown / "Kiểm tra ActivityKit", :400-405) — a stub, not a real check. It affects DiagnosticsV2View, not this cluster, but it is the same runDiagnostics API the redesign might expose more prominently.

5. Default coordinates are hardcoded to Hoàn Kiếm, Hanoi in three places: SimulationCoordinator.swift:28-29, SpoofEngine.swift:42, MainViewV2.swift:18 (camera) and :50-51 (manual-input defaults). RoutineManager's home/work/cafe (:15-21) are likewise hardcoded Hanoi coordinates that ship as sample data and are always drawn on the map.

6. MainViewV2.swift:86 — `if let record = historyManager.stopRecording() { _ = record }` is dead code: the record is discarded, and by the time it runs the app-level sink usually has nothing left to save anyway.

7. MainViewV2.swift:236-244 — the comment says "Toggle 3D pitch" but the implementation is one-way (always pitch 45). Either the comment or the code is wrong; I could not determine which was intended.

THINGS I COULD NOT DETERMINE:
- Whether the duplicate "Route Studio" / "Chạy tuyến" quick actions (both opening RouteStudioView) are a deliberate two-door design or a copy-paste leftover. onStartRoute and onCreateRoute are distinct closure parameters on QuickActionsBar, which suggests they were meant to differ.
- Whether RoutineStudioView being unreachable is a regression or a deliberate soft-disable. There is no #if or comment marking it disabled, and OnboardingViewV2:158 still advertises "Chu trình 24/7 tự động" to new users, which argues it is an accidental regression.
- Whether telemetry.updateRate / distanceTravelledMeters / routeProgress are written by RouteSimulator or ScenarioEngine (outside my cluster). I verified SimulationCoordinator.submit() does not set them; if no other writer exists, three telemetry readouts are permanently dead.

CROSS-CUTTING FOR THE REDESIGN:
- There is no view model anywhere in this cluster. Every view reaches singletons directly from its body. If the new UI keeps that pattern, each of the risks above reappears in the new code.
- Every user-facing string is a hardcoded Vietnamese literal except the six L10n constants used by TelemetryPanel. The L10n enum (AppDesign.swift:250-279) already defines start/stop/pause/resume/retry/diagnostics/noRoutes/noDevice and more — the redesign is the natural moment to route strings through it.
- MainViewV2 mixes injection styles: engine/routine/flight come in as @EnvironmentObject from AutoSpoofVNApp, while coordinator/srManager/deviceManager/historyManager are grabbed as @StateObject singletons — even though the coordinator IS injected as an environment object at AutoSpoofVNApp.swift:43. Pick one convention in the rewrite.
- The app has exactly one root screen and 12 modal sheets, all flag-driven booleans on MainViewV2. If the redesign introduces a tab bar or NavigationStack, every one of the 12 destinations listed in uiEntryPoints needs a new home, and three of them (History, Diagnostics, Shadowrocket, Bypass) currently have two entry points each that should stay reachable.

### Views: RouteStudio + ScenarioStudio + Bookmarks + History

MOCK / STUB / PLACEHOLDER inventory in this cluster (all pre-existing — the redesign must not mistake any of it for real logic, and must not add more):
1. Every ScenarioEditorView "Thêm bước" action inserts hardcoded constants with no editing UI (ScenarioStudioView.swift:168-187): setLocation = Hanoi 21.0285/105.8542, moveTo = 21.03/105.85 @30 km/h, wait = 60 s, dwell = 300 s, randomNearby = 200 m/600 s, changeSpeed = 40 km/h. This is the most placeholder-ish code in the cluster.
2. Import GPX is a stub end-to-end: RouteStudioView.swift:39 sets showImport, RouteStudioView.swift:182 declares it, and nothing observes it. PersistenceManager.importGPX(from:) (PersistenceManager.swift:134-157) is a working regex parser with zero call sites.
3. Export GPX discards its result (`_ = PersistenceManager.shared.exportGPX(...)`, RouteStudioView.swift:264) — the file is written but never surfaced.
4. RouteSimulator.start() contains `let session = coordinator.startSession(...); _ = session // captured for future use` (RouteSimulator.swift:38-39) — an explicit unfinished hook.
5. ScenarioAction.resume and .loop are no-op cases in the engine switch (`break // handled by ...`, ScenarioEngine.swift:148-152) — intentional, since the behaviour lives in pause()/executeSteps(), not dead code.
6. ReplayEngine.seek(to:) (HistoryManager.swift:190-192) only clamps and assigns `progress` — it does NOT move the replay position. Cosmetic stub.
7. ScenarioStudioView.swift:17 reuses L10n.noRoutes ("Chưa có tuyến đường") as the scenario empty-state title — copy placeholder, wrong string.
8. RouteStudioView.swift:8 `@EnvironmentObject var engine: SpoofEngine` — declared, never used.

DEAD / UNREACHABLE API a redesign could cheaply light up (all verified by grep across Sources): ScenarioEngine.resume(), ReplayEngine.pause(), ReplayEngine.resume(), ReplayEngine.seek(to:), HistoryManager.isRecording, RouteSimulator's entire published surface (isRunning/progress/currentSegmentIndex/distanceTravelled/distanceRemaining/eta) and its pause/resume/stop/seek, LocationBookmark.lastUsedAt (written at BookmarksView.swift:105, never read), RouteWaypoint.dwellSeconds (never set or shown by this cluster), SavedRoute.routeGeometry/totalDistanceMeters/estimatedDurationSeconds (never populated), ScenarioStep.order (assigned but never used for execution or re-sorted), RouteProvider.RouteResult.errorDescription / usedFallback / segmentDistancesMeters (never surfaced), PersistenceManager.importGPX.

NO SAVED-ROUTE LIBRARY EXISTS. "Lưu tuyến đường" writes SavedRoutes that no screen can list, load, rename or delete. If the redesign's zero-feature-loss mandate is read literally, this button must be preserved — but it is worth telling the product owner that its output is currently unreachable, and that ScenarioAction.followRoute (the one consumer) can never succeed because routeGeometry is never persisted.

THINGS I COULD NOT VERIFY (not run, iOS-only build on a Windows host — per project rules Xcode work must happen on macOS via `ssh tahoe`):
- Whether Environment(\.dismiss) inside HistoryView pops (when pushed from Settings) or dismisses the Settings sheet. The nested NavigationStack makes the pushed case structurally wrong regardless.
- Whether the tap-to-add-waypoint gesture actually fires for taps that land under the bottom panel overlay (the panel is a sibling in a ZStack and should absorb them, but I did not run it).
- Exact runtime consequences of the unretained RouteSimulator (I traced `[weak self]` at RouteSimulator.swift:41 to a definite no-op loop, but the visible symptom — frozen session vs. immediate completion — is inferred, not observed).

CROSS-FILE COUPLING THE REDESIGN MUST NOT BREAK: LocationSearchView.SearchResult is declared inside RouteStudioView.swift:345-350 and consumed by BookmarksView.swift:138 and :202. RouteStudioView.swift also owns RouteMapView, which nothing else uses. StatusBadge / MetricView / EmptyStateView / CategoryChip are the only shared design components used here (AppDesign.swift:71/119/159 and BookmarksView.swift:111); AppColor/AppFont/AppSpacing/AppRadius tokens are used throughout and are the natural seam for a restyle.

ARBITRATION MODEL THE NEW UI MUST RESPECT: Bookmarks tap -> .manual (priority 100) preempts everything and never auto-releases; scenario -> .scenario (80); Route Studio start -> .route (50); history replay -> .replay (20, loses to everything). Any UI that lets a user tap a bookmark and then expect a replay or scenario to start will hit a silent no-op until stopSession()/halt() releases the manual source.

### Views: WorldTravel + FlightHUD + RoutineStudio + Diagnostics

MOCKS / STUBS / PLACEHOLDERS (intentional — the redesign must neither remove nor mistake these for real logic):

1. USE_IDEVICE_FFI is an OPT-IN build flag. project.yml:105-109 defines it ONLY for the `Debug-FFI` configuration; plain Debug and Release compile the non-FFI branches. So in ordinary builds SpoofEngine (lines 6, 269, 306, 322, 333, 350, 362), SelfPairingManager (15, 114, 268) and DeviceTransport (11, 57, 93, 109, 122, 134) run mock paths, and SpoofEngine sets lastFFIError = "Bản build thiếu thư viện Rust FFI" (SpoofEngine.swift:299). Consequence for this cluster: in a normal build, DiagnosticsV2View's "Transport" self-diagnostic row will read .error / "Chưa kết nối DVT" and the device rows stay empty. That is correct, expected behaviour — not a bug to "fix" in the UI. MockDeviceTransport (DeviceTransport.swift:143) and DeviceTransportType.mock ("Chế độ Kiểm thử (Mock)") are the deliberate test doubles.

2. Hardcoded data that is intentional content, not a stub: AirportRepository's 38 airports (AirportRepository.swift:7-53), FlightManager.worldDestinations' 8 destinations with full sightseeing spot lists (FlightManager.swift:27+), FlightManager.popularAirports' 10-airport duplicate subset (FlightManager.swift:9-26), and RoutineStudioView's 7-row default-schedule reference table (:109-115).

3. Genuine placeholder values a redesign should not present as live telemetry: DeviceManager.transportType = "DVT" (never reassigned); DiagnosticsReport.liveActivityEnabled = true (hardcoded, DeviceManager.swift:126); SimulationCoordinator.runDiagnostics' "Live Activity" row = (.unknown, "Kiểm tra ActivityKit") — it performs no check; FlightPreviewSheet's "Cruise 850 km/h" (WorldTravelViewV2.swift:216) against the engine's actual 800 km/h default.

DEAD CODE FOUND
- FlightHUDView (whole file) — zero call sites repo-wide.
- RoutineStudioView + ScheduleEditorView — sheet declared at MainViewV2.swift:115 but $showRoutineStudio is never set true.
- LocationRow.onUpdate (RoutineStudioView.swift:153) — parameter accepted, never invoked.
- RoutineSchedule / routines.json — persisted by this view only; no engine reads it back.
- DestinationInfo and SightseeingSpotInfo (Models/FlightTypes.swift:21, :44) — a parallel model to WorldDestination/SightseeingSpot with no references anywhere.
- FlightManager.startFlightToDestination / setupDestinationRoutine / startDestinationTour — reachable only indirectly through toggleAutoWorldOdyssey; never called directly by any UI.

DOC ROT WORTH FIXING WHILE REWRITING

The code is self-consistent; only the comment is wrong.

- FlightHUDView.swift:91-93 says FlightPhase is in Models/Types.swift and that FlightManager "defines FlightPhase inline" — the first is true (Types.swift:133), the second is stale (Models/FlightTypes.swift:1-9 repeats the same stale claim).

### Views: ShadowrocketSetup + LocationCloakGuide (BypassTroubleshoot) + DeviceManager + ShadowrocketBanner

FILENAME MISMATCH: LocationCloakGuideView.swift contains only `struct BypassTroubleshootView`. There is no type named LocationCloakGuideView anywhere in the repo (verified by grep across all .swift). Do not go looking for a "location cloak guide" screen — it does not exist.

INTENTIONAL STUBS (must NOT be mistaken for real logic, and must NOT be replaced with new mocks):
- SelfPairingManager.swift:170-174 — the `#else` branch of `#if USE_IDEVICE_FFI` deliberately fails after 1.2s with "Bản build hiện tại đang chạy chế độ mô phỏng, chưa bật FFI thật." So in the default Debug/Release configs, DeviceManagerView's "Ghép nối mới" / "Bắt đầu ghép nối" buttons ALWAYS fail by design.
- SpoofEngine.swift:295-301 — connectLoopback's `#else` branch always sets isLoopbackConnected = false and lastFFIError = "Bản build thiếu thư viện Rust FFI". So DeviceManagerView's "Kết nối" button always fails in mock builds, and the Diagnostics "Thiết bị" row is always red.

- USE_IDEVICE_FFI is set ONLY in the `Debug-FFI` config (project.yml:105-106), reachable only via the AutoSpoofVN-RealFFI scheme on a physical device. The default scheme is AutoSpoofVN-Mock.

- DeviceManager.swift:14 — transportType is the constant "DVT" and is never reassigned; the "Transport" InfoRow is effectively static text.
- CoordinateServer.swift:29-30 — the server's default coordinate is hardcoded Hanoi (21.0285, 105.8542) until SimulationCoordinator first calls updateCoordinate. ShadowrocketSetupView's hero shows coordinator.currentCoordinate instead, so the two can disagree and the UI will not reveal it.

HARD-CODED CONSTANTS THE REDESIGN MUST CARRY OVER VERBATIM:

It is not centralized anywhere. Worth extracting during the rewrite.

### Design layer + Resources + localization + Info.plist + entitlements

SCOPE NOTE: this cluster has no screens of its own, so uiEntryPoints is empty by design. AppDesign.swift and Accessibility.swift are a shared component/token library consumed by other clusters' views; the Resources files are build/runtime configuration. All view-level entry points belong to the MainViewV2 / OnboardingViewV2 clusters.

VERIFIED BY GREP, NOT ASSUMED — the four highest-impact findings:
1. NO asset catalog exists anywhere in the repo (searched for *.xcassets, *.imageset, *.colorset, *.appiconset — zero hits). No app icon, no AccentColor. All iconography is SF Symbols.
2. ZERO localization call sites. Searched for NSLocalizedString, String(localized:), LocalizedStringKey and Bundle.main.localizedString across every .swift file — no matches. The 132 lines of .strings across en/vi are inert. "Localizable" and "lproj" appear nowhere in any .swift, .yml or .md file.
3. NO .preferredColorScheme anywhere, so the Appearance picker in Settings is a no-op.
4. NO BGTaskScheduler reference anywhere, so the plist's BGTaskSchedulerPermittedIdentifiers is dead. SelfPairingManager.registerBackgroundTask() is literally `nonisolated func registerBackgroundTask() {` / `}` at lines 79-80 — an empty body that bootstrap() calls at AutoSpoofVNApp.swift:100.

MOCK / STUB / DEAD-CODE INVENTORY (the redesign must not mistake these for live logic, and must not "fix" them by adding new mocks):
- Intentional build-flag stubs: USE_IDEVICE_FFI guards 16 sites across DeviceTransport.swift, SelfPairingManager.swift and SpoofEngine.swift, plus Vendor/idevice/idevice_stub.c. This is deliberate architecture, documented at project.yml:102-109 and README.md — the default AutoSpoofVN-Mock scheme (config Debug) compiles against the stub so the app runs on a simulator with no real GPS spoofing; only the AutoSpoofVN-RealFFI scheme (config Debug-FFI) links -lidevice_ffi and requires a physical device. NONE of this is in the Design/Resources cluster; the Design layer contains no conditional compilation at all.
- The Design and Resources files themselves are clean of TODO/FIXME/mock/placeholder markers — grep found none.
- True dead code inside this cluster: ErrorBanner (AppDesign.swift:207-245), accessibleButton (Accessibility.swift:6-10), AccessibleMetric (:19-29), ScalableText (:32-46) — all four have zero call sites. Plus 15 of 19 L10n members, 6 of 18 AppColor members (accent, success, overlay, simulating, paused, error), AppSpacing.xxl, and AppRadius.full.
- Hardcoded sample values found at the call sites, worth flagging to the WorldTravel cluster owner: WorldTravelViewV2.swift:216 hardcodes cruise speed "850" km/h into a MetricView, and :218 computes flight time as `distance / 850_000` — a literal constant dressed up as telemetry.

THINGS I COULD NOT RESOLVE (stated as uncertainty, not fact):
- NSBonjourServices declares "_remotepairing-manual-pairing._tcp" (Info.plist:36) but I found no Swift reference to that exact string. SelfPairingManager advertises "_remotepairing-pairable-host._tcp." (:192-194) and browses "_autospoofprobe._tcp" (:230-232). The manual-pairing type may be required by the OS-side RemotePairing handshake rather than by app code — I would NOT remove it without testing on a real device.
- I did not verify that XcodeGen actually emits the .lproj files as a variant group, because the generated .xcodeproj is gitignored and absent, and this is a Windows checkout where xcodegen cannot run. The sources entry at project.yml:66-70 includes the whole AutoSpoofVN folder with only Info.plist excluded, so they should be picked up — but since nothing reads them at runtime, this is moot until localization is actually wired.
- Info.plist has no CFBundleLocalizations key. With .lproj directories present that is normally fine (the bundle derives supported languages from the directories), but it is untested here for the same reason.

REDESIGN GUIDANCE THAT FALLS OUT OF THIS AUDIT:
- The single cheapest high-impact move is to keep every token identifier in AppDesign.swift:6-66 byte-identical and change only the right-hand values. That reskins 543 usage sites across 16 files with one file edit and zero risk of feature loss.
- But token adoption is only partial: 6 AppColor members are unused while views write literal .indigo/.purple/.green in at least 6 places. A pure token swap will leave those hardcoded colours behind, so the redesign needs a second pass over the literal colours listed in the risks.
- The plist comment block at Info.plist:43-48 is a deliberate record that "processing" was removed from UIBackgroundModes as an App Review risk. Do not regress it.

### Engines: RouteProvider + RouteSimulator + ScenarioEngine + MotionEngine + HeadingEngine

MOCK / STUB / PLACEHOLDER AUDIT — none of the five cluster files contain any mock, #if USE_IDEVICE_FFI branch, or simulated data path. They are all real logic. The mock boundary sits strictly downstream, behind SimulationCoordinator.submit() → SpoofEngine: AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Engine/DeviceTransport.swift (USE_IDEVICE_FFI at lines 11, 57, 93, 109, 122, 134; MockDeviceTransport at line 143; a user-selectable "Chế độ Kiểm thử (Mock)" transport at line 18), SelfPairingManager.swift (lines 15, 114, 268) and SpoofEngine.swift (lines 6, 269, 306, 322, 333, 350, 362). Those are intentional, load-bearing stubs for building without the idevice FFI — the redesign must not treat them as dead code, and must not add new ones.

HARDCODED SAMPLE DATA inside the cluster's UI surface (real, shipped, but placeholder-quality): every "Thêm bước" menu item in ScenarioEditorView inserts a fixed constant with no editor — setLocation is pinned to 21.0285/105.8542 (Hanoi), moveTo to 21.03/105.85 @ 30 km/h, wait 60 s, dwell 300 s, randomNearby 200 m/600 s, changeSpeed 40 km/h (ScenarioStudioView.swift:169-186). ScenarioEngine.randomNearby also hardcodes a 3.5 km/h walking speed (ScenarioEngine.swift:172). These are placeholders awaiting a parameter editor, not defaults anyone chose — the redesign should add per-step editing while keeping the same ScenarioAction cases.

THINGS I COULD NOT FULLY DETERMINE:
- Whether RouteSimulator's non-retention is a known regression or was always broken. Git history was not inspected. I verified statically that the only construction site is RouteStudioView.swift:246 and that nothing else stores the instance, and that runSimulationLoop is entered through `Task { [weak self] in await self?.runSimulationLoop() }`. Behaviourally this cannot run, but I did not execute it (no macOS/Xcode available here), so treat it as a very-high-confidence static finding rather than an observed failure.
- Whether RoutineManager and FlightManager (out of my cluster) duplicate any of this motion/heading logic. RoutineManager clearly has its own Timer-driven movement (TimerAudit.swift:11-12) and uses RouteProvider directly rather than RouteSimulator, so there appear to be two parallel route-following implementations. That overlap belongs to whoever audits RoutineManager, but the redesign should decide which one is canonical.

DELIBERATE NO-OPS (do not "fix" during the rewrite): ScenarioAction.resume is `break` with the comment "handled by pause logic" (ScenarioEngine.swift:148-149) and ScenarioAction.loop is `break` with "handled by executeSteps loop" (lines 151-152). The first is genuinely intentional. The second is only half-intentional — the associated `times: Int` really is dropped and the UI prints it, so that one is a defect, not a design.

TEST COVERAGE THE REWRITE MUST NOT BREAK: AutoSpoofVNTests/RouteProviderTests.swift depends on the two public statics RouteProvider.straightLineCoordinates and RouteProvider.resample; AutoSpoofVNTests/SimulationTests.swift depends on MotionEngine.haversineDistance/bearing/moveCoordinate/smoothHeading (awaited, lines 12-47, 222), SpeedProfile.speed(at:progress:) (lines 66-69) and HeadingEngine (line 169). Renaming or de-actor-ing those symbols requires touching the test target.

LATENT FEATURES ALREADY IMPLEMENTED WITH NO UI — free wins if the new design wants them: RouteProvider.diagnosticsSnapshot/resetDiagnostics/clearCache (a routing health panel), RouteSimulator.seek(to:) (a route scrubber — needs the distanceTravelled desync fixed), RouteSimulator's six published telemetry values (route HUD), ScenarioEngine.resume() (the missing resume button), ScenarioAction.changeTravelMode (a travel-mode step), HeadingEngine.cardinalDirection (a localized compass chip), MotionResult.phase (an accelerating/cruising/braking indicator), RoutePlan.segmentDistancesMeters (a per-leg breakdown), and RouteProvider's autoLoop parameter (a loop-route toggle).

ARBITRATION FACTS VERIFIED AGAINST CODE: SimulationSource priorities are exactly as briefed — manual 100, scenario 80, flight 60, route 50, routine 40, replay 20 (SimulationTypes.swift:18-27). acquire() rejects only when a STRICTLY higher-priority source holds control (SimulationCoordinator.swift:66-68), and submit() calls acquire() on every single tick, so a lower-priority engine that lost arbitration silently keeps trying at 10 Hz forever.

### Engines: DeviceManager + DeviceTransport + SelfPairingManager + ConnectionRecovery + CoordinateServer + ShadowrocketManager

MOCK / STUB / PLACEHOLDER CODE (existing and intentional — do not mistake for real logic, and do not add new mocks):

1. `#if USE_IDEVICE_FFI` gating. The flag is set ONLY in the `Debug-FFI` configuration (project.yml:105-109, which also adds `-lidevice_ffi`). The default `AutoSpoofVN-Mock` scheme runs `Debug`, so on simulator and on any machine without Vendor/idevice/libidevice_ffi.a the whole device path is inert:
   - DeviceTransport.swift:83-87 → connect() always returns false with lastError "Bản build thiếu thư viện libidevice_ffi.a"; sendLocation:103-105 and clearLocation:116-118 return false; disconnect:129-131 just flips a bool.
   - SelfPairingManager.swift:170-174 → the entire pairing run is replaced by a 1.2s delay then `.failed("Bản build hiện tại đang chạy chế độ mô phỏng, chưa bật FFI thật.")`. The Bonjour advertising and PIN callbacks (SelfPairingManager.swift:190-212, 268-293) are unreachable in mock builds.
   - SpoofEngine.swift:297-301 → connectLoopback reports "Bản build thiếu thư viện Rust FFI"; sendLocationToDevice/clearDeviceLocation/disconnect become no-ops.
   Consequence for the redesign: in the default scheme every device-connection affordance in this cluster is a dead button that produces a Vietnamese error string. That is intentional, and the new UI must keep those error strings visible rather than hiding the failure.

2. `MockDeviceTransport` (DeviceTransport.swift:143-172) is an intentional deterministic test double (records sentCoordinates) — but it is referenced by nothing, including AutoSpoofVNTests (EngineTests.swift, RouteProviderTests.swift, SimulationTests.swift contain no reference to any file in this cluster).

3. `SelfPairingManager.registerBackgroundTask()` (SelfPairingManager.swift:79-80) has an EMPTY BODY yet is called at bootstrap (AutoSpoofVNApp.swift:78). `import BackgroundTasks` (:8) and `static let taskIdentifier` (:22) are likewise unused, while Info.plist:39-42 still declares BGTaskSchedulerPermittedIdentifiers for "<bundleID>.pairing". This is an abandoned BGTaskScheduler integration, not working code.

4. Hardcoded values presented as live facts: DeviceManager.transportType = "DVT" (never updated), DeviceManager.iosVersion = "" (never assigned), DiagnosticsReport.liveActivityEnabled = true (hardcoded, DeviceManager.swift:126), CoordinateServer /status "running":true (hardcoded, CoordinateServer.swift:194), CoordinateServer default coordinate 21.0285/105.8542 (Hoàn Kiếm) and default accuracy 39.

5. DEAD CODE beyond the mocks: the entire DeviceTransport.swift file (protocol + both conformances + DeviceTransportType); ConnectionRecovery.networkAvailable (published, never read); ConnectionRecovery.cancelRecovery() (no caller); ShadowrocketManager.resetSetup() (no caller); ShadowrocketManager.detectInstallation() has no manual refresh trigger; SelfPairingManager.Phase.connectingTunnel (declared, never assigned); DeviceManager.lastUpdate (written, never read by any view); DeviceManager.reconnect() (no UI caller); TimerAudit.report()/printReport() (debug-only, no UI surface found).

UNCOMMITTED WORKING-TREE CHANGES observed during this audit (another agent appears to be applying compile fixes concurrently; `git status` was clean at session start):
   - CoordinateServer.swift: class-level `@MainActor` removed, re-applied individually to start()/stop()/updateCoordinate(). This is what creates the data race described in risks — flagging it, not reverting it.
   - SimulationCoordinator.swift:326: `sim.currentAltitudeMeters` → `sim.altitudeMeters`.
   - LocationCloakGuideView.swift:58: `coordinator.deviceState == .connected` → `coordinator.deviceState.isConnected` (the old form could never be true, since DeviceConnectionState.connected carries an associated transport String).

THINGS I COULD NOT DETERMINE:
   - Whether ShadowrocketManager's `moduleGitURL` (raw.githubusercontent.com/ndh0408/AutoSpoofVN/main/Proxy/autospoof-location.sgmodule) actually resolves — the file exists locally at Proxy/autospoof-location.sgmodule but I did not verify the repo is public or that the content matches CoordinateServer.moduleResponse().
   - Whether the app-generated module at /module and the checked-in Proxy/autospoof-location.sgmodule + Proxy/location-spoofer.js are in sync; they are maintained separately and could drift.
   - Whether `shadowrocket://install?module=...` is still the correct import scheme for current Shadowrocket versions.
   - Whether `Tweak/LocationCloak/` (a separate jailbreak tweak, and the reason LocationCloakGuideView.swift is named that despite containing `struct BypassTroubleshootView`) is still built or shipped — it is outside this cluster.

FILENAME/TYPE MISMATCH worth fixing during the redesign: `AutoSpoofVN/Sources/Views/LocationCloakGuideView.swift` contains `struct BypassTroubleshootView` and no type named LocationCloakGuideView.

ZERO-FEATURE-LOSS SUMMARY for this cluster — the affordances a new UI must re-wire: DeviceManagerView's 6 buttons (Kết nối, Ngắt kết nối, Test, Xoá GPS, Ghép nối mới, Bắt đầu/Huỷ ghép nối) + Đóng; its 6 info rows and 4 diagnostic rows; the onboarding pairing step's start button + PIN card + success badge; SettingsView's Tự kết nối lại toggle and Heartbeat slider (which must finally be wired); DiagnosticsV2View's device/pairing fields and its JSON + CSV exports; ShadowrocketBanner's single four-way primary button; ShadowrocketSetupView's 3 tabs, Cài Module Live, Cài Module Static, CoordinateServer Khởi động/Dừng toggle, 5 trackable step checkboxes, Reset tiến trình, 2 endpoint cards, 3 test buttons and the Mở Shadowrocket action; BypassTroubleshootView's 3 live checks, 2 issue-card actions and the /coord Safari verify link; and the map-control routes to ShadowrocketSetupView (bolt.horizontal) and BypassTroubleshootView (questionmark.circle) plus the two Settings NavigationLinks. Non-visible behaviours that must also survive: bootstrap ordering in AutoSpoofVNApp.bootstrap(), the willEnterForeground reconnect, the auto-pause/auto-resume recovery loop, the CoordinateServer coordinate sync from BOTH SimulationCoordinator call sites, and ensureVPNActive() firing on every startSession.

### Engines: BackgroundKeeper + LiveActivityManager + AppRecoveryManager + TimerAudit + DeterministicRandom + AppLogger

DEAD CODE / ZERO CALL SITES (verified by repo-wide grep across .swift in AutoSpoofVN, AutoSpoofWidgets, AutoSpoofVNTests):
1. TimerAudit — TimerAudit.report() and printReport() have NO callers anywhere. The whole file is unreachable.
2. DeterministicRandom + SimulationSeedConfig — NO callers anywhere. "Reproducible simulation for QA" does not exist as a feature; do not list it as something to preserve.
3. LiveActivityManager.endActivity() — NO callers. The Live Activity never terminates.
4. BackgroundKeeper.stop() — reachable only through SpoofEngine.stopBackgroundKeepAlive(), which itself has NO callers. Background audio + location run for the whole app lifetime.
5. BackgroundKeeper.requestLocationPermission() and needsSettingsForLocation — NO callers. OnboardingViewV2 (217 lines, 4 steps: welcome / Developer Mode / pairing / ready) never asks for location permission, contradicting BackgroundKeeper's own doc-comment which says onboarding owns that step. The consequence is that the location leg (the ONLY iOS mechanism that can relaunch a killed app) is effectively never armed unless the user grants location for some other reason.

HARDCODED / STUB / PLACEHOLDER CODE FOUND (do not mistake for real logic, and do not "fix" silently):
- DeviceManager.swift:126 — `liveActivityEnabled: true` is hardcoded in DiagnosticsReport; ActivityAuthorizationInfo is never consulted there.
- SimulationCoordinator.swift:401-406 — the "Live Activity" diagnostic row is a literal placeholder: `(.unknown, "Kiểm tra ActivityKit")`, no check performed.
- SystemHealth.background / .simulation / .routing / .liveActivity (SimulationTypes.swift:311-314) are declared and included in `overall` but never assigned anywhere — `health.overall` can never be `.healthy`.
- AppSettings.backgroundKeepAlive (PersistenceManager.swift:98) — persisted, toggled in Settings, read by nothing.
- TimerAudit's doc table (lines 6-32) lists 13 timer owners and asserts "Không có timer nào chạy orphan"; report() covers only 7, and the claim is false given stopBackgroundKeepAlive() is never invoked. Treat the comment as aspirational documentation, not ground truth.

BUILD-CONFIGURATION MOCK BOUNDARY (relevant context, outside this cluster's files): project.yml defines a `Debug-FFI` config that sets SWIFT_ACTIVE_COMPILATION_CONDITIONS USE_IDEVICE_FFI and links -lidevice_ffi, plus two schemes — AutoSpoofVN-Mock (Debug/Release, runs on simulator, GPS does not actually change) and AutoSpoofVN-RealFFI (device-only, needs a real libidevice_ffi.a). NONE of the six files in this cluster contains any `#if USE_IDEVICE_FFI` branch — BackgroundKeeper, LiveActivityManager, AppRecoveryManager, TimerAudit, DeterministicRandom and AppLogger are all real, unconditional code that behaves identically in mock and FFI builds. Vendor/idevice/idevice_stub.c is the mock shim for the non-FFI path but is referenced only with `buildPhase: none`.

INFO.PLIST FACTS THIS CLUSTER DEPENDS ON (AutoSpoofVN/Resources/Info.plist): NSSupportsLiveActivities=true; UIBackgroundModes = ["audio","location"] (the comment there records that "processing" was deliberately removed because no BGTaskScheduler used it); NSLocationWhenInUseUsageDescription and NSLocationAlwaysAndWhenInUseUsageDescription present; BGTaskSchedulerPermittedIdentifiers has $(PRODUCT_BUNDLE_IDENTIFIER).pairing (used by SelfPairingManager.registerBackgroundTask(), not by this cluster). Removing "location" from UIBackgroundModes would make BackgroundKeeper.enableBackgroundLocation() throw an uncatchable ObjC exception.

THINGS I COULD NOT DETERMINE / EXPLICIT UNCERTAINTY:
- I could not verify runtime behaviour (no macOS/Xcode available from this Windows host), so all findings are static-analysis only. Nothing was built or run.
- Whether `AVAudioSession.interruptionNotification` is delivered on the main thread on iOS 17.4+ is not guaranteed by documentation; handleInterruption() mutates @Published isAudioRunning/interruptionCount directly (BackgroundKeeper.swift:130, 133) so it may be a second off-main-publish site in addition to the confirmed one in configureAndPlayAudio(). I flagged only the configureAndPlayAudio path as confirmed.
- AppRecoveryManager.recover() intentionally does not restart the session (the doc-comment says so). I treated that as a design decision, not a bug, but if the redesign is expected to offer true "resume simulation", that capability does not exist today and would be new work.
- SpoofEngine's 20 s heartbeat Timer is created inside startBackgroundKeepAlive() (SpoofEngine.swift:214-231). Its guard is `self.isSimulating`, so it exists but no-ops when idle; because stopBackgroundKeepAlive() is never called it is never invalidated. I did not audit the rest of SpoofEngine — that belongs to another cluster.
- The exported diagnostics files land in the Documents directory but Info.plist declares neither UIFileSharingEnabled nor LSSupportsOpeningDocumentsInPlace, so I believe the user cannot retrieve them; I did not confirm on device.

### Widgets (Live Activity / Dynamic Island) + Persistence + Migration + Tests

MOCKS / STUBS / INTENTIONAL PLACEHOLDERS (do not mistake for real logic, and do not "fix" them):
1. USE_IDEVICE_FFI is the project's deliberate mock boundary. Debug/Release build a mock that runs on simulator; only the Debug-FFI config links -lidevice_ffi and defines the flag (project.yml:102-108). Sixteen `#if USE_IDEVICE_FFI` sites exist in DeviceTransport.swift, SelfPairingManager.swift and SpoofEngine.swift, plus Vendor/idevice/idevice_stub.c which exists precisely so the app links without the real static library. NONE of these are in my cluster's files — the widget, persistence, migration and test files contain zero conditional compilation. Tests are explicitly written to run without FFI ("chạy được không cần thiết bị và không cần thư viện FFI", EngineTests.swift:5).
2. DiagnosticsV2View.swift:401-405 (via SimulationCoordinator.runDiagnostics) hardcodes the Live Activity health row as `.unknown` with the literal text "Kiểm tra ActivityKit". This is a placeholder, not a real check — ActivityAuthorizationInfo().areActivitiesEnabled is available and is already used in LiveActivityManager.swift:25.
3. PersistenceManager.swift:165 has a bare `// Log error in production` comment inside an empty catch — an acknowledged TODO.
4. MigrationManager.swift:56 contains `_ = homeData; _ = workData // acknowledge existence` — a deliberate no-op whose only effect is a log line.
5. RoutineStudioView.swift shows a hardcoded 7-row "Lịch mặc định" reference table (07:00–07:30 Nhà→Công ty … 22:00–07:00 Ngủ). This is static illustrative copy, not data — it is not read from routines.json and does not drive RoutineManager.

DEAD CODE (verified by grep, zero call sites):
- LiveActivityManager.endActivity() — the Live Activity can never be dismissed by the app.
- PersistenceManager.appendHistory(_:) — HistoryManager duplicates the logic inline instead.
- PersistenceManager.importGPX(from:) — the "Import GPX" button (RouteStudioView.swift:39) only flips a boolean that no presenter is bound to.
- MigrationManager.migrateV1toV2's routine-location branch (:52-57).
- AppSettings fields simulationTickRateHz and trailMaxPoints have no UI control at all; nine more fields have controls but no consumer (listed in risks).
- SavedRoute.routeGeometry is never assigned, which makes ScenarioAction.followRoute a permanent no-op.

THINGS I COULD NOT DETERMINE:
- Whether the legacy UserDefaults keys "autospoof_bookmarks", "autospoof_home_location" and "autospoof_work_location" that migrateV1toV2 reads ever existed on a real install. No code in this tree writes them (RoutineManager uses routine_bookmarks / routine_home_lat / routine_home_lon). They may predate the repository, or they may be a genuine bug where the migration targets the wrong keys. I did not delete or reason further; this needs a check against a device that has run an older build.
- Whether the v0→v1 conversion `Data(legacy.utf8)` produces a pairing record the FFI accepts. SpoofEngine.swift:111-114 performs the identical conversion as its own fallback, and SpoofEngine.swift:104 sniffs for a "bplist" prefix on read, which suggests binary plists are the normal case and the UTF-8 path is for XML-text pairing files only.
- I could not execute the test suite — this is a Windows host and the project needs Xcode (project.yml requires xcodeVersion 16.0, iOS 17.4). All pass/fail statements are from reading the code; the one failure I claim (testSmoothHeadingNoJump) I re-derived numerically against the exact algorithm in MotionEngine.swift:129-137 and confirmed it returns 0.0 where the test asserts > 340.

DOCUMENTATION DRIFT: README.md:394-396 lists the test directory as containing only EngineTests.swift and RouteProviderTests.swift — SimulationTests.swift (the largest test file, 233 lines) is missing from the documented tree. Worth correcting during the redesign.

ONE MORE THING THE REDESIGN MUST NOT BREAK: the Live Activity's data contract is the ONLY channel to the widget process. There is no App Group, no shared UserDefaults, no shared container. If the new UI wants richer widget content (route name, ETA, bookmarks, a map snippet), that requires adding an App Group entitlement to BOTH targets in project.yml first — a build-configuration change, not a view change. Similarly, Lock Screen Stop/Pause buttons need a new App Intents target. Both are prerequisites, so they should be planned in the redesign's first milestone rather than discovered in its last.
